Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The integration variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 602 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 35 and intersection form of rank 1.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^35(S, F) is trivial. QED.
